Title: Maxims, Theological Ideas and Sentences, out of the present Ordinary of the Brethren's Churches, his Dissertations and Discourses from the Year 1738 till 1747; extracted by J. Gambold, M.A.; with a Letter from the Author annex'd
Creator: Nikolaus Ludwig, Count von Zinzendorf (1700-1760); extracted and translated by John Gambold (1711-1771)
Publisher: London: sold by J. Beecroft at the Bible and Crown in Lombard Street
Publication Date: 1751
Rights: Public Domain (published 1751, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)

Readable; the long s is rendered as f by the OCR throughout.
Content note: Gambold's anthology of Zinzendorf's sayings, arranged by topic, with the Count's own letter approving the collection; the English Brethren's digest of their Ordinary's thought through the controversial decade.
